0001  000000  %#MODEL nd100
0002  000000  %
0003  000000  % ND-100 MICROCODE, PROM SET 015XX (48 BITS), CPU CARD 3033S
0004  000000  %
0005  000000  % Reconstructed source. The labels, comments and operand names are the ones of the printed
0006  000000  % listing "48-BITS ND-100 (PROM 015XX)" (printout of 16.12.1985, file last written 13.10.1981;
0007  000000  % scan ND100-MICROCODE.pdf, pages 90-218). The instruction words are the ones of the 16 PROM
0008  000000  % chips of the 3033S card: this file assembles with "ndmicroasm nd100-asm" to exactly the chip
0009  000000  % image 3033S-microcode.CODE (2048 words, see REFERENCE/README.md). Where the print and the
0010  000000  % chips differ, the chips win. The differences found:
0011  000000  %   - CYCL: most words print CYCL,FAST (or no CYCL). These PROMs hold the standard (slow)
0012  000000  %     cycle: CYCL,SLOW. CYCL,WAIT is the same in print and chips.
0013  000000  %   - 003514-003517: the print jumps to 5INIT, ENTR, LEAV and ELEAV; the PROMs jump to ILLIN.
0014  000000  %   - Words that are not in the print have no comment and their text is the decoding of the
0015  000000  %     chips: the fill words in front of an origin line (0643-0660, 0731-0737, 1034-1036,
0016  000000  %     1056-1060, 1101-1105, 3450-3457) and the words of the two blank printout pages 165 and
0017  000000  %     191 (2125-2146 and 2772-3004).
0018  000000  % Names: the print writes the register operands in two ways (A,17 and A,R7, B,0 and B,Z); this
0019  000000  % file keeps the way the print writes them where it was read, and the chip decoding elsewhere.
0020  000000  %
0021  000000  %********************************************************************
0022  000000  % 48-BIT N-VERSION
0023  000000  %********************************************************************
0024  000000  % ADDRESS 0 ENTERED AT :
0025  000000  % MASTER CLEAR
0026  000000  % PAGE FAULT
0027  000000  % PROTECT VIOLATION
0028  000000  % ROM OUT OF RANGE
0029  000000  %********************************************************************
0030  000000  0/
0031  000000            A,17 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0032  000000            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0033  000000            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0034  000001            A,15 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0035  000001            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0036  000001            FTCH CONDENABL;
0037  000002  %********************************************************************
0038  000002  % INCREMENT P IF NOT FETCH
0039  000002            B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0040  000002            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0041  000003  FTCH:     A,13 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0042  000003            IDBS,BMG COMM,CLIRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0043  000003            COND,IRQ F,JMP F,HOLD;
0044  000004            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
0045  000004            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0046  000004            NOINT CONDENABL;
0047  000005  %********************************************************************
0048  000005  % INTERRUPT IS PENDING
0049  000005            ALUD,NONE
0050  000005            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0051  000005            COND,STP F,JMP F,HOLD;
0052  000006            ALUD,NONE
0053  000006            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0054  000006            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0055  000007  %********************************************************************
0056  000007  % CPU IS IN STOP MODE, MULTIPLE SINGLE OR BREAKPOINT MUST BE THE CASE
0057  000007            AB,BPFLG ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0058  000007            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0059  000007            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0060  000010            AB,SINGL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0061  000010            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0062  000010            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0063  000011            ALUD,NONE
0064  000011            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0065  000011            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0066  000012  %********************************************************************
0067  000012  % NO INTERRUPT IS PENDING, IT CAN BE MASTER CLEAR OR IOF-PAGEFAULT
0068  000012  NOINT:    ALUD,NONE
0069  000012            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0070  000012            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0071  000013            ALUD,NONE
0072  000013            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0073  000013            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0074  000014  %********************************************************************
0075  000014  % DECREMENT P, IT HAS BEEN INCREMENTED
0076  000014            B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0077  000014            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0078  000014            FTCH2;
0079  000015  %********************************************************************
0080  000015  % EXECUTE LEVEL CHANGE OR JMP *
0081  000015  FTCH2:    PIC,RVECT ALUD,NONE
0082  000015            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0083  000015            FETCH;
0084  000016  %********************************************************************
0085  000016  %********************************************************************
0086  000016  % MAPPING POINT FOR ALL INSTRUCTIONS. NEXT MICROADDRESS IS TAKEN FROM
0087  000016  % MAPPING PROM, OR CONTROLLED BY THE INTERRUPT HARDWARE (INTERRUPT VECTOR)
0088  000016  %********************************************************************
0089  000016  FETCH:    A,P B,Z ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B,YA
0090  000016            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
0091  000017  %********************************************************************
0092  000017  % RETURN FROM P-MODIFYING ROP- OR BOP-INSTRUCTION
0093  000017  PMODX:    ALUD,NONE
0094  000017            IDBS,DSABL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0095  000020  %********************************************************************
0096  000020  % RETURN AFTER JUMPS, OR WHENEVER NEW FETCH OF INSTRUCTION HAS TO BE DONE
0097  000020  INIT:     B,P ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0098  000020            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
0099  000021  %********************************************************************
0100  000021  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN DBR
0101  000021  USDBR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0102  000021            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0103  000021            FETCH;
0104  000022  %********************************************************************
0105  000022  % RETURN AFTER STORE-INSTRUCTIONS, WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0106  000022  STEND:    A,P B,R1 ALUF,A-B ALUD,NONE
0107  000022            IDBS,ALU COMM,ESRP T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0108  000022            COND,R=P F,JMP F,HOLD;
0109  000023            PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0110  000023            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0111  000023            FETCH CONDENABL;
0112  000024  %********************************************************************
0113  000024  % *+1 HAS BEEN CHANGED
0114  000024            PIC,LOSTS B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0115  000024            IDBS,AARG COMM,EPIC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0116  000024            10;
0117  000025            ALUD,NONE
0118  000025            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0119  000025            INIT;
0120  000026  %********************************************************************
0121  000026  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0122  000026  USGPR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0123  000026            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0124  000026            FETCH;
0125  000027  %********************************************************************
0126  000027  % CODE FOR ILLEGAL INTRUCTION
0127  000027  ILLIN:    A,7 ALUD,NONE
0128  000027            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0129  000027            INIT;
0130  000030  %********************************************************************
0131  000030  % CODE FOR PRIVILEGED INSTRUCTION IF ON RING 0-1
0132  000030  PRIVI:    A,11 ALUD,NONE
0133  000030            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0134  000030            INIT;
0135  000031  %********************************************************************
0136  000031  %********************************************************************
0137  000031  %
0138  000031  % STORE INSTRUCTIONS
0139  000031  %
0140  000031  %********************************************************************
0141  000031  STZ:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0142  000031            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0143  000031            MREFS;
0144  000032            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
0145  000032            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0146  000032            STEND;
0147  000033  STA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0148  000033            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0149  000033            MREFS;
0150  000034            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0151  000034            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0152  000034            STEND;
0153  000035  STT:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0154  000035            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0155  000035            MREFS;
0156  000036            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0157  000036            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0158  000036            STEND;
0159  000037  STX:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0160  000037            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0161  000037            MREFS;
0162  000040            A,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0163  000040            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0164  000040            STEND;
0165  000041  STD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B

0186  000050  MIN:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0187  000050            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW

0270  000101            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0271  000101            MREFL;
0272  000102            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SAVE GPR (NEXT INSTR)
0273  000102            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0274  000103            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q  % READ STATUS
0275  000103            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

0277  000104            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW

0280  000105            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0281  000105            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;

0283  000106            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0284  000106            MPY1 CONDENABL;
0285  000107            B,R2 ALUF,-B ALUD,B  % NEGATIVE A-REG.
0286  000107            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0287  000107            COND,COND F,JMP;
0288  000110  MPY1:     B,16 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q  % LOAD LOOP-COUNTER

0775  000327  SETK0:    A,R2 B,Z ALUF,MASKAB ALUD,NONE STS,LO
0776  000327            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0777  000327            USDBR;
0778  000330  %********************************************************************
0779  000330  %********************************************************************
0780  000330  % STORE REGISTER BLOCK

0784  000330            RBLOK;
0785  000331            B,7 ALUD,NONE

0787  000331            SRB1 CONDENABL;

0790  000332            ALUD,NONE
0791  000332            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0792  000332            COND,LC=0;
0793  000333            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE

0797  000335            A,REG B,P ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0798  000336            A,REG B,X ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0799  000337            A,REG B,T ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;

0801  000341            A,REG B,D ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0802  000342            A,REG B,L ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0803  000343            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0804  000343            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT

0808  000344            WRRQ;

0811  000347  %********************************************************************
0812  000347  % SUBROUTINE TO CHECK FOR OWN LEVEL IN SRB, LRB, IRW, IRR
0813  000347  RBLOK:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0814  000347            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

0818  000350            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0819  000351  %********************************************************************

0823  000351            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0824  000352            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
0825  000352            IDBS,ARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0826  000352            170;

0841  000361            A,REG B,A ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0842  000362            A,REG B,D ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0843  000363            A,REG B,L ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;

0959  000424  %********************************************************************

0962  000424            IDBS,ALU MIS,APT COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1149  000515            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1150  000516            A,X B,X ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
1151  000516            IDBS,GPR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1152  000516            USDBR;
1153  000517  %********************************************************************
1154  000517  %********************************************************************
1155  000517  % FLOATING SUBTRACT INSTRUCTION
1156  000517  %********************************************************************
1157  000517  FSB:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1158  000517            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1159  000517            MREFL;
1160  000520            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1161  000520            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1162  000521  %********************************************************************
1163  000521  % INVERT SIGN OF MEM. OPERAND
1164  000521            A,17 B,R2 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,B
1165  000521            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1166  000521            FAD2;
1167  000522  %********************************************************************
1168  000522  %********************************************************************
1169  000522  % FLOATING ADD ROUTINE
1170  000522  %********************************************************************
1171  000522  FAD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1172  000522            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1173  000522            MREFL;
1174  000523            B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1175  000523            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1176  000524  %********************************************************************
1177  000524  % COMMON PART OF FAD & FSB
1178  000524  FAD2:     B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1179  000524            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1180  000524            2;
1181  000525            A,5 B,R6 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1182  000525            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1183  000526            A,17 B,R5 ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1184  000526            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1185  000527            A,R5 B,T ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,Q
1186  000527            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1187  000530            A,R2 B,R5 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,B
1188  000530            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1189  000530            RDRQ;
1190  000531            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SAVE GPR (NEXT INSTR)
1191  000531            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1192  000532            AB,OCTNR ALUF,Q-A ALUD,B  % COMPARE EXPONENTS
1193  000532            T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1194  000532            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1195  000533            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
1196  000533            IDBS,STS COMM,CLFF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1197  000533            NED1 CONDENABL;
1198  000534            A,R5 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1199  000534            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1200  000534            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1201  000535            A,R5 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,NONE
1202  000535            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1203  000535            EQUAL CONDENABL;
1204  000536  %********************************************************************
1205  000536  % MEMORY OPERAND HAS SMALLEST EXPONENT, SHIFT DOWN MANTISSA
1206  000536            A,R6 B,R5 ALUF,MASKAB ALUD,NONE
1207  000536            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1208  000536            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1209  000537  %********************************************************************
1210  000537  % TEST IF MEMORY OPERAND INSIGNIFICANT, ANSWER = TAD
1211  000537            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1212  000537            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1213  000537            STSST CONDENABL;
1214  000540            ALUD,NONE
1215  000540            IDBS,DSABL T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1216  000540            RDRQ;
1217  000541            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1218  000541            IDBS,DBR COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1219  000541            COND,LC=0;
1220  000542  %********************************************************************
1221  000542  % SHIFT DOWN MEMORY OPERAND MANTISSA
1222  000542            B,R3 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD STS,ES
1223  000542            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,LOAD LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW
1224  000542            DUMM2;
1225  000543            ALUD,NONE
1226  000543            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1227  000543            SUBF CONDENABL;
1228  000544  %********************************************************************
1229  000544  % EQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS, ADD MANTISSAS
1230  000544  ADDF:     A,D B,D ALUF,A+Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1231  000544            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1232  000545  ADDF2:    A,R3 B,A ALUF,A+B ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1233  000545            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1234  000545            COND,CRY F,JMP F,HOLD;
1235  000546            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1236  000546            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1237  000546            TGOOD CONDENABL;
1238  000547  %********************************************************************
1239  000547  % ADDITION GAVE CARRY, SHIFT MANTISSA RIGHT
1240  000547            ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1241  000547            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1242  000547            1;
1243  000550            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1244  000550            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1245  000551            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1246  000551            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1247  000552            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1248  000552            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1249  000552            40000;
1250  000553            A,T ALUF,A+1 ALUD,Q  % CHECK OVERFLOW
1251  000553            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1252  000553            CHKOF;
1253  000554            A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1254  000554            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1255  000554            STSEX;
1256  000555  %********************************************************************
1257  000555  % TAD-OPERAND HAS SMALLEST EXPONENT, SHIFT DOWN MANTISSA
1258  000555  NED1:     A,R5 B,R4 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
1259  000555            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1260  000556            A,R6 B,R4 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,Q
1261  000556            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1262  000557            ALUF,INVQ ALUD,NONE
1263  000557            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1264  000557            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1265  000560  %********************************************************************
1266  000560  % TEST IF TAD-OPERAND INSIGNIFICANT, ANSWER = MEMORY OPERAND
1267  000560            A,R5 ALUF,A+1 ALUD,NONE
1268  000560            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1269  000560            NORM2 CONDENABL;
1270  000561  %********************************************************************
1271  000561  % EXCHANGE OPERANDS
1272  000561            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1273  000561            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1274  000562            A,A B,R3 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1275  000562            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1276  000563            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1277  000563            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1278  000563            RDRQ;
1279  000564            B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1280  000564            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1281  000565            A,R4 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1282  000565            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1283  000566            A,R5 B,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1284  000566            IDBS,ALU COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1285  000566            COND,LC=0;
1286  000567  %********************************************************************
1287  000567  % SHIFT DOWN TAD-OPERAND
1288  000567            B,R3 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD STS,ES
1289  000567            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,LOAD LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW
1290  000567            DUMM2;
1291  000570            A,R2 B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1292  000570            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1293  000570            SUBF CONDENABL;
1294  000571            A,D B,D ALUF,A+Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1295  000571            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1296  000571            ADDF2;
1297  000572  %********************************************************************
1298  000572  % ANSWER = MEMORY OPERAND
1299  000572  NORM2:    B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1300  000572            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1301  000572            RDRQ;
1302  000573            B,D ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1303  000573            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1304  000574            A,R2 B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1305  000574            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1306  000574            STSST;
1307  000575  %********************************************************************
1308  000575  % DIFFERENT SIGNS ON OPERANDS, SUBTRACT MANTISSAS
1309  000575  SUBF:     A,D B,D ALUF,A-Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1310  000575            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1311  000576            A,R3 B,A ALUF,B-A-1 ALUD,B CRY,C
1312  000576            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1313  000576            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1314  000577            A,STS ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1315  000577            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1316  000577            FAD3 CONDENABL;
1317  000600            B,STS ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B  % SET TG-FLIP-FLOP
1318  000600            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1319  000600            2;
1320  000601            A,D B,D ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1321  000601            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1322  000602  FAD3:     A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1323  000602            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1324  000602            NOINV;
1325  000603  %********************************************************************
1326  000603  % EQUAL EXPONENTS
1327  000603  EQUAL:    A,R2 B,T ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE
1328  000603            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1329  000603            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1330  000604            ALUD,NONE
1331  000604            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1332  000604            SUBF3 CONDENABL;
1333  000605  %********************************************************************
1334  000605  % EQUAL SIGNS, ADD MANTISSAS
1335  000605            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1336  000605            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1337  000605            RDRQ;
1338  000606            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1339  000606            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1340  000606            ADDF;
1341  000607  %********************************************************************
1342  000607  % DIFFERENT SIGNS, SUBTRACT MANTISSAS
1343  000607  SUBF3:    B,R3 ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1344  000607            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1345  000607            RDRQ;
1346  000610            A,D ALUF,A-D-1 ALUD,Q STS,EA
1347  000610            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1348  000611            A,R3 B,A ALUF,A+B ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1349  000611            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1350  000611            COND,CRY F,JMP F,HOLD;
1351  000612            ALUD,NONE
1352  000612            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1353  000612            INV CONDENABL;
1354  000613            ALUF,Q+1 ALUD,Q STS,EA
1355  000613            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1356  000614            B,A ALUF,B ALUD,B CRY,C
1357  000614            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1358  000614            NOINV;
1359  000615  % INVERT ANSWER AFTER SUBTRACTION
1360  000615  INV:      ALUF,INVQ ALUD,Q
1361  000615            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1362  000615            100000;
1363  000616            A,T B,T ALUF,XORDA ALUD,B
1364  000616            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1365  000617            B,A ALUF,INVB ALUD,B
1366  000617            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1367  000620  %********************************************************************
1368  000620  % NORMALIZE ANSWER AFTER SUBTRACTION
1369  000620  NOINV:    B,R4 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1370  000620            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1371  000620            21;
1372  000621            A,2 B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
1373  000621            IDBS,AARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1374  000621            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1375  000622            A,T B,R5 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1376  000622            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1377  000622            FAD4 CONDENABL;
1378  000623            A,T B,R5 ALUF,A-D ALUD,B
1379  000623            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1380  000624            B,A ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1381  000624            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1382  000624            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1383  000625            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q
1384  000625            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1385  000625            FAD4 CONDENABL;
1386  000626  %********************************************************************
1387  000626  % ANSWER = 0
1388  000626  ZTAD:     B,A ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1389  000626            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1390  000627            B,D ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1391  000627            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1392  000630            A,R7 B,T ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B,YA
1393  000630            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1394  000630            STSEX;
1395  000631  % CONTINUED NORMALIZE AFTER SUBTRACTION
1396  000631  FAD4:     A,16 B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1397  000631            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1398  000631            COND,F15;
1399  000632            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
1400  000632            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1401  000633            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1402  000633            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1403  000634            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1404  000634            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1405  000635            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1406  000635            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1407  000636            A,R4 B,LC ALUF,A-D-1 ALUD,Q
1408  000636            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1409  000637            A,R5 ALUF,A-Q ALUD,Q  % CHECK IF UNDERFLOW
1410  000637            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1411  000637            CHKUF;
1412  000640            A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % RESTORE GPR
1413  000640            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1414  000640            STSEX;
1415  000641  % SUBROUTINE TO CORRECT FOR OVERSHOOT AFTER MANTISSA ALIGNMENT
1416  000641  DUMM2:    B,R3 MIS,LIN ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
1417  000641            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1418  000642            A,R2 B,T ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE
1419  000642            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1420  000642            COND,F15 F,NEXT;
1421  000643            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1422  000643            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1423  000644            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1424  000644            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1425  000645            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1426  000645            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1427  000646            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1428  000646            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1429  000647            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1430  000647            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;

1446  000657            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1447  000660            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1448  000660            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1449  000661  %********************************************************************
1450  000661  %********************************************************************
1451  000661  % FLOATING MULTIPLY ROUTINE
1452  000661  %********************************************************************
1453  000661  FMU:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1454  000661            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1455  000661            MREFL;
1456  000662            B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1457  000662            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1458  000662            2;

1460  000663            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1465  000665            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1467  000666            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1469  000667            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1470  000667            RDRQ;
1471  000670            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,B
1472  000670            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1473  000670            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1474  000671            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1475  000671            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1476  000671            FMU2 CONDENABL;
1477  000672  %********************************************************************
1478  000672  % UNEQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS
1479  000672            A,17 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1480  000672            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1481  000673  FMU2:     ALUD,NONE
1482  000673            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1483  000673            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1484  000674            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1485  000674            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1486  000674            40000;

1489  000676            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XNORAQ ALUD,B

1493  000677            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1494  000700  %********************************************************************
1495  000700  % OFUF MEANS EITHER OVER- OR UNDER-FLOW AFTER EXPONENT ADD
1496  000700            ALUD,NONE
1497  000700            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1498  000700            OFUF CONDENABL;
1499  000701  %********************************************************************
1500  000701  % MULTIPLY MANTISSAS
1501  000701  FMU1:     A,16 B,T ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,B
1502  000701            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1508  000703            17;

1510  000704            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1511  000705            A,A B,R1 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD STS,EA CRY,C
1512  000705            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1513  000706            A,R3 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1514  000706            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1515  000707            ALUD,NONE
1516  000707            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1517  000707            16;
1518  000710            A,D ALUF,A+Q ALUM,FMU ALUD,Q STS,EA
1519  000710            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1520  000711            A,A B,R1 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD STS,EA CRY,C
1521  000711            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1522  000712            A,D ALUF,A+Q ALUM,FMU ALUD,Q STS,EA
1523  000712            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1524  000713            A,A B,R1 ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1525  000713            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1526  000713            COND,CRY F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1527  000714            A,R1 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1528  000714            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1529  000714            MCRY1 CONDENABL;
1530  000715  %********************************************************************
1531  000715  % NO CARRY IN LAST ADDITION
1532  000715            A,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1533  000715            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1534  000715            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1535  000716            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1536  000716            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1537  000716            ZTAD CONDENABL;
1538  000717            A,T ALUF,A-1 ALUD,Q  % CHECK UNDERFLOW
1539  000717            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1540  000717            CHKUF;
1541  000720  %********************************************************************
1542  000720  % CHECK TG-BEHAVIOUR
1543  000720  TGOOD:    ALUD,NONE
1544  000720            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1545  000720            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1546  000721  TGTST:    A,STS ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1547  000721            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1548  000721            STSST CONDENABL;
1549  000722  TGSET:    B,STS ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B  % SET TG
1550  000722            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1551  000722            2;
1552  000723            A,D B,D ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1553  000723            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1554  000723            STSST;
1555  000724  %********************************************************************
1556  000724  % THERE WAS CARRY FROM LAST ADDITION IN MULTIPLY LOOP
1557  000724  MCRY1:    B,1 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1558  000724            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1559  000724            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1560  000725  %********************************************************************
1561  000725  % SHIFT DOWN MANTISSAS
1562  000725            A,1 B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1563  000725            IDBS,BMG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1564  000725            FMU3 CONDENABL;
1565  000726            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1566  000726            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1567  000727            ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1568  000727            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1569  000727            1;
1570  000730  FMU3:     A,R7 B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B,YA  % RESTORE GPR
1571  000730            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1572  000730            STSEX;
1573  000731            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1574  000731            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1575  000732            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1576  000732            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1577  000733            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1578  000733            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1579  000734            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1580  000734            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1581  000735            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1582  000735            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1583  000736            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1584  000736            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1585  000737            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1586  000737            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1587  000740  %********************************************************************
1588  000740  %********************************************************************
1589  000740  % FLOATING DIVIDE ROUTINE
1590  000740  %********************************************************************
1591  000740  FDV:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1592  000740            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1593  000740            MREFL;
1594  000741            B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1595  000741            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1596  000741            2;
1597  000742            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
1598  000742            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1599  000743            B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1600  000743            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1601  000743            RDRQ;
1602  000744  %********************************************************************
1603  000744  % SUBTRACT EXPONENTS
1604  000744            A,T B,R2 ALUF,A-B ALUD,Q
1605  000744            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1606  000745            A,17 ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
1607  000745            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1608  000746            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,B
1609  000746            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1610  000746            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1611  000747            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1612  000747            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1613  000747            FDV2 CONDENABL;
1614  000750  %********************************************************************
1615  000750  % UNEQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS
1616  000750            A,17 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1617  000750            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1618  000751  FDV2:     B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1619  000751            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1620  000751            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1621  000752            A,16 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,Q
1622  000752            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1623  000752            FLOVF CONDENABL;
1624  000753            B,R1 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
1625  000753            IDBS,ALU MIS,HOLD COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1626  000754            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1627  000754            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1628  000754            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1629  000755            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1630  000755            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1631  000755            40000;
1632  000756            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1633  000756            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1634  000757            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAQ ALUD,B
1635  000757            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1636  000757            FDV3 CONDENABL;
1637  000760            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1638  000760            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1639  000761            ALUD,NONE
1640  000761            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1641  000761            FDV3 CONDENABL;
1642  000762  %********************************************************************
1643  000762  % OVERFLOW OR UNDERFLOW IN FLOATING OPERATION
1644  000762  OFUF:     A,T B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1645  000762            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1646  000762            COND,F=0 F,HOLD;
1647  000763            ALUD,NONE
1648  000763            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1649  000763            FLOVF CONDENABL;
1650  000764            ALUD,NONE
1651  000764            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1652  000764            ZTAD;
1653  000765  %********************************************************************
1654  000765  % DIVIDE MANTISSAS
1655  000765  FDV3:     B,T ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1656  000765            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1657  000765            16;
1658  000766            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1659  000766            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1660  000766            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1661  000767            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1662  000767            IDBS,ALU COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1663  000767            ZTAD CONDENABL;
1664  000770            A,7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE STS,LO
1665  000770            IDBS,BMG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1666  000771            A,R4 ALUF,Q-A ALUD,Q STS,EA
1667  000771            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1668  000772            A,R3 B,A MIS,ZIN ALUF,B-A-1 ALUD,SLD STS,ES CRY,C
1669  000772            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1670  000772            COND,LC=0;
1671  000773            A,R4 ALUF,Q-A-1 ALUM,FDV ALUD,Q STS,EA CRY,GPR
1672  000773            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1673  000774            A,R3 B,A MIS,ZIN ALUF,B-A-1 ALUM,FDV ALUD,SLD STS,ES CRY,C
1674  000774            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1675  000775            B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1676  000775            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1677  000776            A,R5 B,17 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1678  000776            IDBS,BARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
1679  000777            A,R4 ALUF,Q-A-1 ALUM,FDV ALUD,Q STS,EA CRY,GPR
1680  000777            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1681  001000            A,R3 B,A MIS,ZIN ALUF,B-A-1 ALUM,FDV ALUD,SLD STS,ES CRY,C
1682  001000            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1683  001001            A,R5 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1684  001001            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1685  001001            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1686  001002  %********************************************************************
1687  001002  % CHECK IF NORMALIZATION IS NECESSARY
1688  001002            B,D ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1689  001002            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1690  001002            POSDV CONDENABL;
1691  001003            A,T ALUF,A+1 ALUD,Q  % CHECK OVERFLOW
1692  001003            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1693  001003            CHKOF;
1694  001004            ALUD,NONE
1695  001004            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1696  001004            COND,DZD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1697  001005  %********************************************************************
1698  001005  % CHECK TG-BEHAVIOUR
1699  001005  TGTN2:    A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1700  001005            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1701  001005            DZD0 CONDENABL;
1702  001006  DZD1:     A,D B,D ALUF,MASKDA ALUD,NONE  % SET TG
1703  001006            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1704  001007            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1705  001007            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1706  001007            2;
1707  001010            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ORAQ ALUD,B
1708  001010            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1709  001011  STSEX:    A,STS ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE STS,LO
1710  001011            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1711  001011            USGPR;
1712  001012  DZD0:     A,D B,D ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1713  001012            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1714  001012            STSEX;
1715  001013  %********************************************************************
1716  001013  % NORMALIZE ANSWER IN FLOATING DIVIDE
1717  001013  POSDV:    A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1718  001013            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1719  001014            B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
1720  001014            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1721  001014            COND,DZD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1722  001015            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1723  001015            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1724  001015            TGTN2;
1725  001016  %********************************************************************
1726  001016  % FLOATING OVERFLOW
1727  001016  FLOVF:    B,T ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B
1728  001016            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1729  001016            77777;
1730  001017            A,Z B,A ALUF,INVA ALUD,B
1731  001017            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1732  001020            A,Z B,D ALUF,INVA ALUD,B
1733  001020            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1734  001021            B,10 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1735  001021            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1736  001022            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ORAQ ALUD,B
1737  001022            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1738  001023  STSST:    A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1739  001023            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1740  001023            STSEX;
1741  001024  %********************************************************************
1742  001024  % CHECK IF FLOATING UNDERFLOW
1743  001024  CHKUF:    A,T B,R2 ALUF,MASKAQ ALUD,B
1744  001024            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1745  001024            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,POP;
1746  001025            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1747  001025            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1748  001026            B,T ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1749  001026            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1750  001026            ZTAD CONDENABL;
1751  001027  %********************************************************************
1752  001027  % CHECK IF FLOATING OVERFLOW
1753  001027  CHKOF:    B,R2 ALUF,INVQ ALUD,B
1754  001027            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1755  001030            A,T B,R2 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,B
1756  001030            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1757  001030            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1758  001031            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1759  001031            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1760  001032            B,T ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1761  001032            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1762  001032            CONDENABL;
1763  001033            A,17 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,Q
1764  001033            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1765  001033            FLOVF;
1766  001034            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1767  001034            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1768  001035            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1769  001035            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1770  001036            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1771  001036            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1772  001037  %********************************************************************
1773  001037  %********************************************************************
1774  001037  % NORMALIZE INSTRUCTION
1775  001037  %********************************************************************
1776  001037  NLZ:      A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1777  001037            T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1778  001037            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1779  001040            B,D ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1780  001040            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1781  001040            NLZ1 CONDENABL;
1782  001041            B,T ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B  % ANSWER =0
1783  001041            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1784  001041            USDBR;
1785  001042  NLZ1:     ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1786  001042            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1787  001042            40000;
1788  001043            B,T ALUF,D+Q ALUD,B
1789  001043            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1790  001044            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1791  001044            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1792  001044            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1793  001045            A,17 ALUD,NONE
1794  001045            IDBS,BMG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1795  001045            NLZ2 CONDENABL;
1796  001046  %********************************************************************
1797  001046  % A<0
1798  001046            A,T B,T ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
1799  001046            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1800  001047            B,A ALUF,-B ALUD,B
1801  001047            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1802  001050  NLZ2:     B,R4 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1803  001050            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1804  001050            21;
1805  001051            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLB  % NORMALIZE LOOP
1806  001051            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1807  001052            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRB
1808  001052            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1809  001053            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRB
1810  001053            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1811  001054            A,R4 B,LC ALUF,A-D-1 ALUD,Q
1812  001054            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1813  001055            A,T B,T ALUF,A-Q ALUD,B
1814  001055            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1815  001055            USDBR;
1816  001056            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1817  001056            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1818  001057            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1819  001057            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1820  001060            T,JMP0-3 T,MAPJ T,HOLD LCOUNT SLWCS CYCL,SLOW
1821  001060            CONDENABL COND,DR=2 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1822  001061  %********************************************************************
1823  001061  %********************************************************************
1824  001061  % DENORMALIZE INSTRUCTION
1825  001061  %********************************************************************
1826  001061  DNZ:      A,T ALUF,D+A
1827  001061            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1828  001061            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1829  001062            B,R2 ALUF,D+Q ALUD,B
1830  001062            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1831  001062            20;
1832  001063            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1833  001063            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1834  001063            40000;
1835  001064            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1836  001064            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1837  001065            A,16 B,D ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
1838  001065            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1839  001065            DNZ1 CONDENABL;
1840  001066  %********************************************************************
1841  001066  % ANSWER = 0
1842  001066            B,T ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1843  001066            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1844  001067            B,A ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1845  001067            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1846  001070            B,D ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1847  001070            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1848  001070            USDBR;
1849  001071  DNZ1:     ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
1850  001071            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1851  001071            RDIVZ CONDENABL;
1852  001072  %********************************************************************
1853  001072  % NOT OVERFLOW
1854  001072            ALUD,NONE
1855  001072            IDBS,DSABL T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
1856  001072            COND,LC=0;
1857  001073            B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SRD
1858  001073            T,NEXT T,NEXT MIS,APT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
1859  001074            B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
1860  001074            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1861  001075            B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
1862  001075            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1863  001076            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1864  001076            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1865  001076            COND,F15 F,JMP F,POP;
1866  001077            B,T ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1867  001077            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW

2665  001452  %********************************************************************
2666  001452  % REMOVE CURRENT PID-BIT
2667  001452            B,R1 ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
2668  001452            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2669  001453            AB,PID ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2670  001453            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2671  001454            A,LC ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
2672  001454            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2673  001455            A,R1 B,16 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE
2674  001455            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2675  001455            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2676  001456            AB,PID ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2677  001456            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2678  001456            WT14 CONDENABL;
2679  001457  %********************************************************************
2680  001457  % NOT ON LEVEL 14
2681  001457            A,R1 B,12 ALUF,A-D ALUD,Q
2682  001457            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2683  001457            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2684  001460            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2685  001460            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2686  001460            CHKIT CONDENABL;
2687  001461  %********************************************************************
2688  001461  % ON LEVEL 10-15 (EXCEPT 14)
2689  001461            B,4 ALUF,Q-D ALUD,NONE
2690  001461            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2691  001461            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2692  001462            A,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2693  001462            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2694  001462            W1013 CONDENABL;
2695  001463  %********************************************************************
2696  001463  % ON LEVEL 15
2697  001463            A,17 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2698  001463            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2699  001464  W1013:    PIC,MCLPID ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2700  001464            IDBS,ALU COMM,EPIC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2701  001465  %********************************************************************
2702  001465  %********************************************************************
2703  001465  % ROUTINE TO CHECK WHICH INTERRUPT LEVEL SHOULD BE ENTERED
2704  001465  %********************************************************************
2705  001465  CHKIT:    PIC,LOSTS B,10 ALUD,NONE
2706  001465            IDBS,BARG COMM,EPIC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2707  001465            COND,IRQ F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2708  001466            AB,PID ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2709  001466            IDBS,REG COMM,CLIRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2710  001467            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
2711  001467            IDBS,STS T,RETURN T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2712  001467            CONDENABL COND,F15 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
2713  001470  %********************************************************************
2714  001470  % NO HARDWARE INTERRUPT REQUEST IS PENDING
2715  001470            AB,PIE ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,SLB
2716  001470            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2717  001470            CONDENABL COND,F11;
2718  001471  %********************************************************************
2719  001471  % CPU IS IN ION
2720  001471            ALUF,PASSD
2721  001471            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
2722  001471            100013;
2723  001472  %********************************************************************
2724  001472  % LOOP TO FIND HIGHEST ENABLED LEVEL
2725  001472            B,R3 ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
2726  001472            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
2727  001473            B,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q  % NEW LEVEL -> Q
2728  001473            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2729  001473            PLINT;
2730  001474  %********************************************************************
2731  001474  % THE WAIT-INSTRUCTION WAS ON LEVEL 14
2732  001474  WT14:     ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2733  001474            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2734  001474            40000;
2735  001475            PIC,MCLPID ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2736  001475            IDBS,ALU COMM,EPIC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2737  001475            CHKIT;
2738  001476  %********************************************************************
2739  001476  %********************************************************************
2740  001476  % HARDWARE INTERRUPT HANDLING (EXT. OR INT. INTERRUPTS)
2741  001476  % ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3740-3757)
2742  001476  %********************************************************************
2743  001476  % INTERNAL INTERRUPTS
2744  001476  EXT14:    B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
2745  001476            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2746  001476            27;
2747  001477            A,16 ALUD,NONE
2748  001477            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2749  001500  %********************************************************************
2750  001500  % ONLY LEVEL 15 IS HIGHER THAN STATUS 27
2751  001500            PIC,LOSTS ALUD,NONE
2752  001500            IDBS,GPR COMM,EPIC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2753  001500            PLINT;
2754  001501  %********************************************************************
2755  001501  % HARDWARE INTERRUPT EXCEPT LEVEL 14
2756  001501  XINT:     B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
2757  001501            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2758  001502  %********************************************************************
2759  001502  % LEVEL IN Q SHOULD BE ENTERED
2760  001502  PLINT:    ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2761  001502            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2762  001502            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2763  001503            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2764  001503            IDBS,SWAP T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2765  001503            PLV0 CONDENABL;
2766  001504  %********************************************************************
2767  001504  % NEW LEVEL IS NOT LEVEL 0, UPDATE PID
2768  001504            A,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2769  001504            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2770  001505            AB,PID ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B
2771  001505            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2772  001506            AB,PID ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
2773  001506            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2774  001507  PLV0:     A,PIL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2775  001507            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2776  001510            A,LC ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
2777  001510            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2778  001510            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
2779  001511            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
2780  001511            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2781  001511            140000 CONDENABL;
2782  001512  %********************************************************************
2783  001512  % NEW LEVEL IS UNEQUAL TO OLD LEVEL
2784  001512            A,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2785  001512            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2786  001512            COND,COND F,JMP F,HOLD;
2787  001513            A,PIL B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2788  001513            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2789  001513            LVSWP CONDENABL;
2790  001514  %********************************************************************
2791  001514  % OLD LEVEL IS NOT 14 OR 15
2792  001514            AB,PVL ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
2793  001514            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2794  001515  %********************************************************************
2795  001515  % CHANGE LEVEL ROUTINE. Q = NEW LEVEL
2796  001515  LVSWP:    AB,ACTLV ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B
2797  001515            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2798  001516            ALUD,NONE
2799  001516            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2800  001516            7;
2801  001517            B,Z ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2802  001517            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
2803  001517            COND,LC=0;
2804  001520            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSB ALUD,Q  % SAVE LOOP
2805  001520            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
2806  001521            B,Z ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
2807  001521            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
2808  001521            170000;

2810  001522            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDPIL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2811  001523            ALUD,NONE

2813  001523            7;

2817  001525            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
2818  001526            A,Z ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE STS,LO
2819  001526            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2820  001526            INIT;

2824  001527            37760;

3058  001637            B,STS ALUD,NONE  % -1 -> REG.FILE
3059  001637            IDBS,ARG COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3060  001637            177777;

3063  001640            ST77;

3069  001642            10000;

3072  001643            A,17 B,STS ALUF,D+1 ALUD,NONE

3074  001643            STERR CONDENABL;

3078  001644            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3079  001644            STER1 CONDENABL;
3080  001645            A,17 B,STS ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3081  001645            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3082  001646  %********************************************************************
3083  001646  % STER2 IF REG.FILE NOT 0 (6000)
3084  001646            ALUD,NONE
3085  001646            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3086  001646            STER2 CONDENABL;
3087  001647            A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE STS,LO
3088  001647            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDPIL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3089  001650            A,A B,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
3090  001650            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
3091  001651  %********************************************************************
3092  001651  % TEST 8 : TEST OF PIC
3093  001651  ST8:      B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3094  001651            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3095  001651            6400;
3096  001652            ALUD,NONE  % PID -> REG.FILE
3097  001652            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3098  001652            TAPID;
3099  001653            A,17 B,STS ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3100  001653            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3101  001654            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q  % 0 -> PID
3102  001654            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,LOAD CYCL,SLOW
3103  001654            TRPID;
3104  001655            ALUD,NONE  % 176000 -> PID
3105  001655            IDBS,ARG COMM,SMPID T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3106  001655            140017;
3107  001656            ALUD,NONE
3108  001656            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3109  001656            TAPID;
3110  001657            ALUF,D-Q ALUD,Q  % CHECK PID
3111  001657            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,SLOW
3112  001657            176000;
3113  001660            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3114  001660            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3115  001660            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3116  001661  %********************************************************************
3117  001661  % STERR IF PID NOT 176000 (6400)
3118  001661            A,17 B,STS ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3119  001661            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3120  001661            STERR CONDENABL;
3121  001662            B,R6 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B  % REG.FILE -> PID
3122  001662            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3123  001662            TRPID;
3124  001663            A,R7 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B

3126  001663            MACL2;

3128  001664  % SUBROUTINE TO PLACE IDB HIGH
3129  001664  ST77:     ALUD,NONE
3130  001664            IDBS,ARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
3131  001664            177777;
3132  001665  %********************************************************************
3133  001665  % DISPLAY ERROR NO. R2+2
3134  001665  STER2:    A,R2 B,R2 ALUF,A+B ALUD,B
3135  001665            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3136  001665            400;
3137  001666  %********************************************************************
