3140  001666            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3141  001666            400;
3142  001667  %********************************************************************
3143  001667  % DISPLAY ERROR NO. R2
3144  001667  STERR:    B,R2 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,Q
3145  001667            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3146  001667            DYTP2;
3147  001670            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE  % LOOP ON ERROR
3148  001670            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDPIL T,RETURN T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3149  001671  %********************************************************************
3150  001671  % LAST PART OF MASTER CLEAR ROUTINE
3151  001671  MACL2:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3152  001671            IDBS,ARG COMM,SIOC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3153  001671            30140;
3154  001672            AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3155  001672            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3156  001673            AB,MANIR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3157  001673            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3158  001674            AB,TXT1 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3159  001674            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3160  001675            AB,TXT2 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3161  001675            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3162  001676            AB,SINGL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q
3163  001676            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3164  001676            LVSWP;
3165  001677  %********************************************************************
3166  001677  %********************************************************************
3167  001677  % SUBROUTINE USED BY MOPC AND MACL TO CLEAR SEVERAL MOPC VARIABLES
3168  001677  %********************************************************************
3169  001677  SPAC2:    AB,DUMPF ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT CYCL,SLOW;
3170  001700            AB,SCRAM ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3171  001701            AB,OCTNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3172  001702            AB,CURNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3173  001703            AB,UPPNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3174  001704            AB,OCTN2 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3175  001705            AB,DEPOS ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;
3176  001706  %********************************************************************
3177  001706  %********************************************************************
3178  001706  % STOP CPU. ENTERED FROM WAIT-INSTRUCTION. PRINT #
3179  001706  %********************************************************************
3180  001706  STOPW:    AB,PRCHR ALUD,NONE
3181  001706            IDBS,ARG COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3182  001706            443;
3183  001707  %********************************************************************
3184  001707  %********************************************************************
3185  001707  % FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR IF THE CPU IS IN STOP (3760 OR 3770)
3186  001707  %********************************************************************
3187  001707  STOP:     AB,MACL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3188  001707            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3189  001707            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3190  001710            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3191  001710            IDBS,IOR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3192  001710            STOP2 CONDENABL;
3193  001711  %********************************************************************
3194  001711  % STOP FOLLOWS DIRECTLY AFTER MASTER CLEAR. STAND-BY IS NOT OK
3195  001711            A,R5 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3196  001711            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3197  001711            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3198  001712            AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3199  001712            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3200  001712            STOP2 CONDENABL;
3201  001713  %********************************************************************
3202  001713  % PANEL IS LOCKED WHEN POWER-UP, OR LOAD IS PRESSED
3203  001713  ALDLO:    ALUD,NONE  % USE ALD-SWITCH
3204  001713            IDBS,ALU T,JMPALD T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3205  001713            ALDVC;
3206  001714  %********************************************************************
3207  001714  % LOAD, $ OR &, LOAD CODE IN Q
3208  001714  LOAD1:    A,17 ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
3209  001714            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3210  001714            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3211  001715            A,15 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3212  001715            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3213  001715            INIT CONDENABL;
3214  001716  %********************************************************************
3215  001716  % Q IS NOT 0
3216  001716            A,7 B,STS ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
3217  001716            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3218  001716            ETLO1 CONDENABL;
3219  001717  %********************************************************************
3220  001717  %********************************************************************
3221  001717  % MASS STORAGE LOAD, BECAUSE BIT 13 IS 1
3222  001717  %********************************************************************
3223  001717  MASS:     A,1 B,D ALUF,D+Q ALUD,B  % DEV.NO. +2 -> D
3224  001717            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3225  001720  MAS1:     B,A ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3226  001720            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3227  001721  %********************************************************************
3228  001721  % IOX N+1 (0) CORE ADDRESS
3229  001721            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
3230  001721            T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3231  001721            IOXG;
3232  001722  %********************************************************************
3233  001722  % IOX N+1 (0) CORE ADDRESS
3234  001722            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
3235  001722            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3236  001722            IOXG;
3237  001723  %********************************************************************
3238  001723  % IOX N+3 (0) BLOCK ADDRESS
3239  001723            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A+1 ALUD,B
3240  001723            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3241  001723            IOXG;
3242  001724  %********************************************************************
3243  001724  % IOX N+7 (2000) WORD COUNTER
3244  001724            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
3245  001724            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3246  001724            5;
3247  001725            A,12 B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3248  001725            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3249  001725            IOXG;
3250  001726  %********************************************************************
3251  001726  % IOX N+5 (4) ACTIVATE DEVICE
3252  001726            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A+1 ALUD,B
3253  001726            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3254  001727            A,2 B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3255  001727            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3256  001727            IOXG;
3257  001730  %********************************************************************
3258  001730  % IOX N+4 READ STATUS
3259  001730  MAS2:     A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
3260  001730            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3261  001730            IOXG;
3262  001731            A,A B,4 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
3263  001731            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3264  001731            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3265  001732            A,2 B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3266  001732            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3267  001732            MAS2 CONDENABL;
3268  001733  %********************************************************************
3269  001733  % DEVICE FINISHED
3270  001733            A,A B,R7 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
3271  001733            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3272  001734            ALUD,NONE
3273  001734            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3274  001734            MAS1 CONDENABL;
3275  001735  %********************************************************************
3276  001735  % NO ERRORS, START IN 0, AND LEAVE MOPC
3277  001735            B,P ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3278  001735            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3279  001735            ESCAP;
3280  001736  %********************************************************************
3281  001736  %********************************************************************
3282  001736  % RESTART, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3766)
3283  001736  % ONLY IF STAND-BY POWER IS OK, OR RESTART BUTTON PRESSED
3284  001736  %********************************************************************
3285  001736  RSTRT:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3286  001736            IDBS,IOR COMM,CLRTC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3287  001736            COND,STP F,RETURN F,POP;
3288  001737            A,15 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
3289  001737            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3290  001737            CONDENABL COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3291  001740  %********************************************************************
3292  001740  % ONLY IF IN STOP MODE
3293  001740            AB,MACL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3294  001740            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3295  001740            RSTR1 CONDENABL;
3296  001741  %********************************************************************
3297  001741  % IF LOCKED
3298  001741            AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3299  001741            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMPALD T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3300  001741            ALDVC;
3301  001742            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3302  001742            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3303  001742            COND,F15 F,NEXT;
3304  001743  %********************************************************************
3305  001743  % LOAD IF BIT 15=1, START IN ADDRESS 20 IF BIT 15=0
3306  001743            ALUD,NONE
3307  001743            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3308  001743            LOAD1 CONDENABL;
3309  001744  %********************************************************************
3310  001744  % START CPU IN ADDRESS 20
3311  001744  RSTR2:    A,2 B,P ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3312  001744            IDBS,AARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3313  001744            CONT;
3314  001745  %********************************************************************
3315  001745  % IF UNLOCKED, DO NOTHING JUST AFTER MASTER CLEAR, ELSE START IN 20
3316  001745  RSTR1:    AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3317  001745            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3318  001745            INIT CONDENABL;
3319  001746            ALUD,NONE
3320  001746            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3321  001746            RSTR2;
3322  001747  %********************************************************************
3323  001747  %********************************************************************
3324  001747  % LOAD BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3764 OR 3774)
3325  001747  %********************************************************************
3326  001747  LOAD:     ALUD,NONE
3327  001747            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3328  001747            COND,STP F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3329  001750            ALUD,NONE
3330  001750            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3331  001750            ALDLO CONDENABL;
3332  001751  %********************************************************************
3333  001751  %********************************************************************
3334  001751  % CPU IS NOT IN STOP WHEN LOAD IS PRESSED, OR
3335  001751  % CONTINUE BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3765 OR 3775)
3336  001751  %********************************************************************
3337  001751  CONT:     ALUD,NONE
3338  001751            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3339  001751            ESCAP;
3340  001752  %********************************************************************
3341  001752  %********************************************************************
3342  001752  % $ OR & IS TYPED TO MOPC
3343  001752  %********************************************************************
3344  001752  DOLOA:
3345  001752  ETLOA:    AB,OCTNR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3346  001752            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3347  001752            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3348  001753  %********************************************************************
3349  001753  % USE ALD-SWITCH IF NO OCTAL NUMBER GIVEN
3350  001753            ALUD,NONE
3351  001753            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3352  001753            ALDLO CONDENABL;
3353  001754            ALUD,NONE
3354  001754            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3355  001754            LOAD1;
3356  001755  %********************************************************************
3357  001755  %********************************************************************
3358  001755  % BINARY LOADER. LOAD CODE BIT 13 IS 0.
3359  001755  %********************************************************************
3360  001755  ETLO1:    B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B  % DEV.NO. -> D
3361  001755            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3362  001755            DVACT;
3363  001756  SEEK:     A,R5 B,P ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % POSSIBLE S.A.
3364  001756            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3365  001757  SIKI:     A,5 B,L ALUF,D+1 ALUD,B  % READ OCTAL NO.
3366  001757            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3367  001757            ASS8;
3368  001760            A,R2 B,15 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE
3369  001760            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3370  001760            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3371  001761            A,R2 B,L ALUF,A-B ALUD,NONE
3372  001761            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3373  001761            SEEK CONDENABL;
3374  001762  %********************************************************************
3375  001762  % LAST CHAR. IS NOT CR
3376  001762            ALUD,NONE
3377  001762            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3378  001762            EXFOU CONDENABL;
3379  001763  %********************************************************************
3380  001763  % LAST CHAR. IS NOT !
3381  001763            ALUD,NONE
3382  001763            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3383  001763            SIKI;
3384  001764  %********************************************************************
3385  001764  % EXCLAMATION MARK (!) FOUND
3386  001764  EXFOU:    A,STS B,STS ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
3387  001764            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3388  001765            B,L ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B  % READ CORE ADDR.
3389  001765            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3390  001765            BIN;
3391  001766            A,Z B,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % READ WORD COUNT
3392  001766            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3393  001766            BIN;
3394  001767            A,Z B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
3395  001767            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3396  001770  %********************************************************************
3397  001770  % LOOP TO STORE PROGRAM IN MEMORY
3398  001770  STLP:     ALUD,NONE  % READ ONE WORD
3399  001770            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3400  001770            BIN;
3401  001771            B,T ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B  % DECR. WORD COUNT
3402  001771            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3403  001771            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3404  001772            A,X B,X ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B,YA  % INCR. CORE ADDR.
3405  001772            IDBS,ALU MIS,PT COMM,WRRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3406  001772            COND,COND F,JMP F,HOLD;
3407  001773            A,Z B,L ALUF,A+B ALUD,B,YA  % ACCUMULATE CH.SM.
3408  001773            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
3409  001773            STLP CONDENABL;
3410  001774  %********************************************************************
3411  001774  % ALL WORDS ARE PLACED IN MEMORY
3412  001774            ALUD,NONE  % READ CHECKSUM
3413  001774            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3414  001774            BIN;
3415  001775            A,Z B,L ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE  % TEST CH.SM.
3416  001775            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3417  001775            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3418  001776            ALUD,NONE
3419  001776            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3420  001776            ILLEG CONDENABL;
3421  001777  %********************************************************************
3422  001777  % CHECKSUM OK, READ ACTION CODE
3423  001777            ALUD,NONE
3424  001777            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3425  001777            ASS8;
3426  002000            A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
3427  002000            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3428  002000            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3429  002001            ALUD,NONE
3430  002001            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3431  002001            ESCAP CONDENABL;
3432  002002  %********************************************************************
3433  002002  % ACTION CODE =0, START PROGRAM
3434  002002            ALUD,NONE
3435  002002            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3436  002002            ESCAP;
3437  002003  %********************************************************************
3438  002003  % SUBROUTINE FOR BINARY LOADER TO READ ONE CHARACTER
3439  002003  INCH:     A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B  % IOX N+2
3440  002003            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3441  002003            IOXG;
3442  002004            A,A B,10 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
3443  002004            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3444  002004            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3445  002005            ALUD,NONE
3446  002005            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3447  002005            INCH CONDENABL;
3448  002006  %********************************************************************
3449  002006  % READY FOR TRANSFER
3450  002006            A,D B,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % IOX N
3451  002006            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3452  002006            IOXG;
3453  002007            A,A B,STS ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
3454  002007            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3455  002007            DVACT;
3456  002010  %********************************************************************
3457  002010  % ACTIVATE DEVICE
3458  002010  DVACT:    B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3459  002010            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3460  002010            4005;
3461  002011            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A+1 ALUD,B  % IOX N+3 (4005)
3462  002011            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3463  002011            IOXG;
3464  002012  %********************************************************************
3465  002012  % SUBROUTINE FOR BINARY LOADER TO READ OCTAL NUMBER, RETURNS ON NON-OCTAL CHAR
3466  002012  ASS8:     AB,OCTNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3467  002012            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3468  002012            ASS81;
3469  002013  %********************************************************************
3470  002013  % OCTAL DIGIT FOUND
3471  002013  ASS82:    ALUD,NONE
3472  002013            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3473  002013            OCDIG;
3474  002014  ASS81:    AB,OCTNR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % READ CHARACTER
3475  002014            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3476  002014            INCH;
3477  002015            A,STS B,R2 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
3478  002015            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3479  002015            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3480  002016            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3481  002016            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3482  002016            60;
3483  002017            A,R2 B,R3 ALUF,A-Q ALUD,B
3484  002017            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3485  002020            A,R3 B,10 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE
3486  002020            IDBS,BARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
3487  002020            CONDENABL COND,F15 F,RETURN F,POP;
3488  002021            ALUD,NONE
3489  002021            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3490  002021            ASS82 CONDENABL;
3491  002022  %********************************************************************
3492  002022  % SUBROUTINE USED BY BINARY LOADER TO READ TWO BYTES AS ONE 16-BIT WORD
3493  002022  BIN:      ALUD,NONE  % READ BYTE 1
3494  002022            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3495  002022            INCH;
3496  002023            ALUD,NONE  % SWAP BYTES
3497  002023            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3498  002024            B,Z ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3499  002024            IDBS,SWAP T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3500  002024            INCH;
3501  002025            A,Z B,Z ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B  % MAKE WORD
3502  002025            IDBS,GPR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;
3503  002026  %********************************************************************
3504  002026  %********************************************************************
3505  002026  % ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR EVERY 20 MS
3506  002026  %********************************************************************
3507  002026  MS20:     B,10 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3508  002026            IDBS,BARG COMM,CLRTC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3510  002027            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3511  002027            WSIOC;
3512  002030  %********************************************************************
3513  002030  % TEST IF 20 MS CLOCK SHOULD ACTIVATE MOPC
3514  002030            ALUD,NONE
3515  002030            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDIR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3516  002030            150040;
3517  002031            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3518  002031            IDBS,MMR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3519  002031            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3520  002032            ALUD,NONE
3521  002032            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3522  002032            MOPC CONDENABL;
3523  002033  %********************************************************************
3524  002033  % MOPC SHOULD BE DRIVEN BY PANEL REQUEST FROM MEMORY MANAGEMENT MODULE
3525  002033            ALUD,NONE
3526  002033            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3527  002033            INIT;
3528  002034  %********************************************************************
3529  002034  %********************************************************************
3530  002034  % PANEL REQUEST, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3762)
3531  002034  %********************************************************************

3533  002034            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDIR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3534  002034            150040;
3535  002035            ALUD,NONE
3536  002035            IDBS,MMR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3538  002036  %********************************************************************

3543  002036            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW

3547  002037            NOUTP CONDENABL;

3550  002040            A,16 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE

3555  002041            OUTPT CONDENABL;

3557  002042  % OUTPUT STOPPED BY INPUT

3559  002042            IDBS,ALU COMM,RSDA T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3560  002042            RONLY;
3561  002043  OUTPT:    ALUD,NONE
3562  002043            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3563  002043            INIT CONDENABL;
3564  002044  %********************************************************************
3565  002044  % UART READY FOR OUTPUT
3566  002044            AB,PRCHR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3567  002044            IDBS,REG COMM,TBSTR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3568  002044            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;

3570  002045            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3571  002045            43;

3574  002046            B,12 ALUF,D-Q ALUD,NONE
3575  002046            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3576  002046            CR CONDENABL;

3578  002047  % JUMP TO LFEED IF OUTPUTTED CHAR. WAS LF
3579  002047            AB,PRCHR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE

3581  002047            LFEED CONDENABL;

3587  002051            OUTP1 CONDENABL;

6069  003545  % MOPC STOP-ROUTINE CONTINUED
6070  003545  STOP2:    AB,SINGL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
6071  003545            IDBS,REG COMM,SSTOP T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
6072  003545            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;

6080  003547            SINGL;

6091  003552            SINGL CONDENABL;

6096  003553            RONLY;
